Composition and biochemical capacity do not establish food-specific clinical efficacy.

BRS5-FM2-PM5 — Resistant starch

Food target and evidence

Input = Resistant starch

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Resistant starch reaching the colon supplies fermentable carbohydrate to microbial communities capable of its utilisation, supporting SCFA-production capacity.

Evidence source = review/brs5-pm5-retest-2026-10-23/report.md

Limitation = Utilisation and product distribution depend on starch structure, processing, accessibility, primary degraders and cross-feeding partners. Evidence for one formulation does not establish an equivalent butyrate response from another preparation or whole food. No predictable yield, intake target or brain benefit is established. Cultivar and ripening state change the result. No amount is inferred from total carbohydrate or fibre. Verified class composition does not establish a formulation-specific butyrate response.

Dietary input = Yes

BRS5-FM2-PM5 — Pectin

Food target and evidence

Apples (studied cultivars) → Pectin: food ontology link pending.

Input = Pectin

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Fermentable polysaccharide supplying microbial SCFA production in human-microbiota culture experiments.

Evidence source = review/brs5-pm5-retest-2026-10-23/report.md

Limitation = Pectin structure, purified preparation, donor community and enriched culture medium affect interpretation. Pascale is secondary synthesis; Bang is bounded primary culture evidence. Whole apples are not equivalent to the tested citrus preparation; no human brain or clinical benefit is established. Qualitative supply only; retain cultivar, fraction and food-matrix variation. No per-100-g pectin amount inferred from total fibre; whole fruit is not purified citrus pectin. Apple pectin remains provisional, not a tested whole-apple butyrate intervention.

Dietary input = Yes

Apples (studied cultivars)ⓘ — Cultivar-specific extracted analytical fractions; not a standardised pectin dose; material: Analytical cell-wall pectin fractions from the thirteen apple cultivars studied by Liu et al.; contains dietary input; Pectin structure, purified preparation, donor community and enriched culture medium affect interpretation. Pascale is secondary synthesis; Bang is bounded primary culture evidence. Whole apples are not equivalent to the tested citrus preparation; no human brain or clinical benefit is established. Qualitative supply only; retain cultivar, fraction and food-matrix variation. No per-100-g pectin amount inferred from total fibre; whole fruit is not purified citrus pectin. Apple pectin remains provisional, not a tested whole-apple butyrate intervention. Composition source

BRS1-FM4-PM9 — Vitamin B6

Food target and evidence

Input = Vitamin B6

Input type = cofactor precursor

Biological role = Dietary B6 vitamers enter salvage/activation pathways that supply PLP for glutamate decarboxylase; pathway details depend on the vitamer.

Evidence source = https://www.uniprot.org/uniprotkb/Q99259; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17766369/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/15182361/

Limitation = Dietary B6 provision of PLP precursors is supported, but increasing B6 intake has not been shown to increase human brain GABA synthesis; PLP supports many enzymes and is not GABA-specific. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin B6 in Bananas, raw. Dietary B6 supports PLP-dependent GAD capacity. Kinase phosphorylation and PNPO oxidation are distinct; no intake-to-human-brain-synthesis efficacy claim. PDXK phosphorylates B6 vitamers; phosphorylated pyridoxine/pyridoxamine require PNPO. Canonical intermediate records are missing; no complete chain or conversion yield was fabricated.

BRS2-FM1-PM1 — Vitamin B12 (cobalamin)

Food target and evidence

Input = Vitamin B12 (cobalamin)

Input type = cofactor precursor

Biological role = Dietary cobalamin is absorbed and processed intracellularly to the methylcobalamin cofactor used by methionine synthase.

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30693532/

Limitation = Provision of vitamin B12 does not show that increasing intake raises tissue remethylation flux. Added vitamin B12 further lowered blood homocysteine beyond folic acid in pooled trials, which remains a circulating marker. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin B12 in Beef, ground, 85% lean meat / 15% fat, raw (Includes foods for USDA's Food Distribution Program). Dietary cobalamin requires absorption and intracellular processing; neither all algal corrinoids nor unverified products are reliable active B12 sources.

Dietary input = Yes

Input = Vitamin B12 (cobalamin)

Input type = cofactor precursor

Biological role = Dietary cobalamin is absorbed and processed intracellularly to the methylcobalamin cofactor used by methionine synthase.

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30693532/

Limitation = Provision of vitamin B12 does not show that increasing intake raises tissue remethylation flux. Added vitamin B12 further lowered blood homocysteine beyond folic acid in pooled trials, which remains a circulating marker. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin B12 in Milk, whole, 3.25% milkfat, with added vitamin D. Dietary cobalamin requires absorption and intracellular processing; neither all algal corrinoids nor unverified products are reliable active B12 sources.

Dietary input = Yes

Milk — preparation unresolved; supplies precursor; Provision of vitamin B12 does not show that increasing intake raises tissue remethylation flux. Added vitamin B12 further lowered blood homocysteine beyond folic acid in pooled trials, which remains a circulating marker. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin B12 in Milk, whole, 3.25% milkfat, with added vitamin D. Dietary cobalamin requires absorption and intracellular processing; neither all algal corrinoids nor unverified products are reliable active B12 sources. Composition source

BRS3-FM2-PM5 — Vitamin E

Food target and evidence

Input = Vitamin E

Input type = nutrient/substance

Biological role = Chain-breaking antioxidant that limits lipid-peroxyl radical propagation in membranes

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17561088/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/804799/

Limitation = Vitamin E breaks peroxyl-radical chains in membranes. That chemistry does not show that extra vitamin E lowers peroxidation in people. A prevention trial found more prostate cancers with 400 IU a day. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin E in Nuts, almonds. Alpha-tocopherol-related lipid protection is a capacity relationship, not general antioxidant efficacy or a high-dose recommendation.

Dietary input = Yes

Almonds — preparation unresolved; contains dietary input; Vitamin E breaks peroxyl-radical chains in membranes. That chemistry does not show that extra vitamin E lowers peroxidation in people. A prevention trial found more prostate cancers with 400 IU a day. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin E in Nuts, almonds. Alpha-tocopherol-related lipid protection is a capacity relationship, not general antioxidant efficacy or a high-dose recommendation. Composition source

BRS4-FM1-PM1 — Iron

Food target and evidence

Input = Iron

Input type = cofactor

Biological role = Iron–sulfur clusters and haem irons transfer electrons along the respiratory complexes.

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/34621061/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/27509854/

Limitation = This identifies iron centres in the chain. It does not show that eating more iron, once iron status is adequate, raises electron transport. USDA FoodData Central reports iron in Nuts, almonds. Iron centres support electron transfer; extra iron after adequacy has no established performance benefit.

Dietary input = Yes

Almonds — preparation unresolved; contains dietary input; This identifies iron centres in the chain. It does not show that eating more iron, once iron status is adequate, raises electron transport. USDA FoodData Central reports iron in Nuts, almonds. Iron centres support electron transfer; extra iron after adequacy has no established performance benefit. Composition source

Dietary input = Yes

Limitation = The review does not show that eating more riboflavin, once flavin mononucleotide (FMN) is adequate, raises electron transport. Riboflavin-responsive complex I disease is a separate clinical context. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin B2 in Nuts, almonds. Riboflavin supplies the FMN cofactor through endogenous activation; not a second independent need for oral FMN.

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/24470588/; https://www.nature.com/articles/nature13241

Limitation = Diet can provide DHA for membrane incorporation. That does not establish an ADHD treatment effect or a human dosing rule from the porcine phospholipid comparison. DHA presence is supported by the authorised algal-oil specifications on this page. The amount is formulation-specific and is not established as one value for algal oil in general. Dietary DHA supplies membrane-incorporation capacity; lipid transport and remodelling intervene. Piglet formulation results do not establish superiority of ordinary fish/roe in people.

Limitation = Diet can provide DHA for membrane incorporation. That does not establish an ADHD treatment effect or a human dosing rule from the porcine phospholipid comparison. USDA FoodData Central reports DHA in Mollusks, oyster, eastern, wild, raw. Dietary DHA supplies membrane-incorporation capacity; lipid transport and remodelling intervene. Piglet formulation results do not establish superiority of ordinary fish/roe in people.

Dietary input = Yes

That does not establish an ADHD treatment effect or a human dosing rule from the porcine phospholipid comparison. USDA FoodData Central reports DHA in Mollusks, oyster, eastern, wild, raw. BRS1-FM2-PM6 — Choline

Food target and evidence

Input = Choline

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Direct substrate combined with acetyl-CoA by choline acetyltransferase to form acetylcholine

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/10594838/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/36840817/

Limitation = Choline is required and diet supplies it, but additional intake has not been shown here to raise acetylcholine synthesis in the human brain or improve attention. USDA FoodData Central reports choline in Broccoli, raw. Choline is a ChAT substrate; delivery, neuronal uptake and local homeostasis do not imply extra-intake cognitive benefit.

BRS1-FM2-PM6 — Phosphatidylcholine

Food target and evidence

Input = Phosphatidylcholine

Input type = substrate provision

Biological role = Dietary choline-containing phospholipid that is digested and remodelled, producing circulating choline as one route to the direct choline substrate

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/10594838/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/36840817/

Limitation = Human tracer evidence establishes phosphatidylcholine as a choline-provision route; it does not show delivery to cholinergic terminals or increased brain acetylcholine synthesis. Reported in Mytilus mussel edible tissue. Food PC supplies a choline source after digestion/remodelling; no ordinary-food equivalence to the labelled supplement response.

Dietary input = Yes

Reported in Mytilus mussel edible tissue. Food PC supplies a choline source after digestion/remodelling; no ordinary-food equivalence to the labelled supplement response. Composition source

BRS2-FM1-PM2 — Betaine

Food target and evidence

Input = Betaine

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Methyl-donor substrate for BHMT remethylation of homocysteine to methionine

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12220488/

Limitation = Supplemental betaine lowers plasma homocysteine in healthy adults, but that change does not measure tissue BHMT activity or show a clinical benefit. Human evidence is an eight-man depletion pilot, with the challenge response in four clinically depleted participants; tissue BHMT flux was not measured. Rat donor rescue and specific inhibition support the chain. This does not establish ordinary-diet limitation, response in every tissue or benefit above adequacy. Folate’s indirect constraint on BHMT remains unresolved. Raw beets contained 130 mg betaine per 100 g in the corrected USDA choline database. That assay is separate from this page’s other nutrients. Dietary betaine supplies the BHMT methyl donor; plasma homocysteine changes are not measured BHMT flux.

Dietary input = Yes

Beetroot — raw; contains dietary input; Supplemental betaine lowers plasma homocysteine in healthy adults, but that change does not measure tissue BHMT activity or show a clinical benefit. Human evidence is an eight-man depletion pilot, with the challenge response in four clinically depleted participants; tissue BHMT flux was not measured. Rat donor rescue and specific inhibition support the chain. This does not establish ordinary-diet limitation, response in every tissue or benefit above adequacy. Folate’s indirect constraint on BHMT remains unresolved. Raw beets contained 130 mg betaine per 100 g in the corrected USDA choline database. That assay is separate from this page’s other nutrients. Dietary betaine supplies the BHMT methyl donor; plasma homocysteine changes are not measured BHMT flux.

That assay is separate from this page’s other nutrients. Dietary betaine supplies the BHMT methyl donor; plasma homocysteine changes are not measured BHMT flux.

BRS2-FM1-PM2 — Betaine

Food target and evidence

Input = Betaine

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Methyl-donor substrate for BHMT remethylation of homocysteine to methionine

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12220488/

Limitation = Supplemental betaine lowers plasma homocysteine in healthy adults, but that change does not measure tissue BHMT activity or show a clinical benefit. Human evidence is an eight-man depletion pilot, with the challenge response in four clinically depleted participants; tissue BHMT flux was not measured. Rat donor rescue and specific inhibition support the chain. This does not establish ordinary-diet limitation, response in every tissue or benefit above adequacy. Folate’s indirect constraint on BHMT remains unresolved. Frozen spinach preparations contained 110–130 mg betaine per 100 g in the corrected USDA database. This page records raw spinach, so that figure is not its composition panel. Dietary betaine supplies the BHMT methyl donor; plasma homocysteine changes are not measured BHMT flux.

BRS2-FM1-PM2 — Choline

Food target and evidence

Input = Choline

Input type = precursor

Biological role = Dietary precursor that can be oxidised to betaine, the methyl-donor substrate for BHMT

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/12220488/; https://doi.org/10.1016/j.abb.2013.07.018; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/36840817/

Limitation = Choline oxidation can supply betaine, but increasing choline intake has not been shown to increase tissue BHMT flux. Human evidence is an eight-man depletion pilot, with the challenge response in four clinically depleted participants; tissue BHMT flux was not measured. Rat donor rescue and specific inhibition support the chain. This does not establish ordinary-diet limitation, response in every tissue or benefit above adequacy. Folate’s indirect constraint on BHMT remains unresolved. USDA FoodData Central reports choline in Broccoli, raw. Choline can supply betaine after sequential oxidation, especially liver/kidney; this is not a brain BHMT or dose-effect claim.

Dietary input = Yes

Broccoli — raw; supplies precursor; Choline is required and diet supplies it, but additional intake has not been shown here to raise acetylcholine synthesis in the human brain or improve attention. USDA FoodData Central reports choline in Broccoli, raw. Choline is a ChAT substrate; delivery, neuronal uptake and local homeostasis do not imply extra-intake cognitive benefit. Composition source

BRS4-FM1-PM3 — Creatine

Food target and evidence

Input = Creatine

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Creatine accepts and donates phosphate through the reversible creatine-kinase reaction, forming the phosphocreatine reserve used for rapid ATP buffering.

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/10484486/

Limitation = The brain also synthesises creatine, and exogenous exposure produces smaller and less consistent cerebral responses than skeletal-muscle responses. Contains creatine; amounts vary with the cut and cooking. Creatine supports creatine/phosphocreatine buffering; brain exposure responses are variable and supplement studies do not prove meat-based brain effects.

Dietary input = Yes

Beef — raw; contains dietary input; The brain also synthesises creatine, and exogenous exposure produces smaller and less consistent cerebral responses than skeletal-muscle responses. Contains creatine; amounts vary with the cut and cooking. Creatine supports creatine/phosphocreatine buffering; brain exposure responses are variable and supplement studies do not prove meat-based brain effects. Composition source

BRS4-FM1-PM2 — Nicotinamide (a form of vitamin B3)

Food target and evidence

Chicken (studied breast material) → Nicotinamide (a form of vitamin B3): food ontology link pending.

Input = Nicotinamide (a form of vitamin B3)

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Nicotinamide is the salvage substrate that NAMPT recycles toward NAD⁺.

Evidence source = https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7963035/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32386566/

Limitation = The review identifies the salvage substrate. It does not show that eating more nicotinamide, once intake is adequate, raises NAD⁺. The myopathy niacin study used nicotinic acid at pharmacological doses. No numerical amount, free-vitamer pool, cooking yield, bioavailability or food-to-NAD effect inferred. Total niacin is not relabelled as either form. Nicotinamide supports NAD salvage capacity; food assay pools and bioavailability are qualified independently.

Dietary input = Yes

Chicken (studied breast material)ⓘ — Laboratory acid extraction at 121 °C; not a cooking recommendation or native-free-vitamer measurement; material: Chicken breast purchased in Turkey; cooking state not established; contains dietary input; The review identifies the salvage substrate. It does not show that eating more nicotinamide, once intake is adequate, raises NAD⁺. The myopathy niacin study used nicotinic acid at pharmacological doses. No numerical amount, free-vitamer pool, cooking yield, bioavailability or food-to-NAD effect inferred. Total niacin is not relabelled as either form. Nicotinamide supports NAD salvage capacity; food assay pools and bioavailability are qualified independently. Composition source

BRS4-FM1-PM2 — Nicotinic acid (a form of vitamin B3)

Food target and evidence

Chicken (studied breast material) → Nicotinic acid (a form of vitamin B3): food ontology link pending.

Input = Nicotinic acid (a form of vitamin B3)

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Nicotinic acid is the Preiss–Handler substrate that cells convert toward NAD⁺.

Evidence source = https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7963035/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32386566/

Limitation = In five adults with mitochondrial myopathy, niacin at 750–1,000 mg/day raised blood NAD⁺ and brought muscle NAD⁺ to the control level. That is medical dosing in a deficiency disease, not a result for adequate everyday intake, and attention was not measured. No numerical amount, free-vitamer pool, cooking yield, bioavailability or food-to-NAD effect inferred. Total niacin is not relabelled as either form. Nicotinic acid supports NAD precursor capacity; myopathy supplement responses are not transferred to ordinary foods or brain outcomes.

Dietary input = Yes

Chicken (studied breast material)ⓘ — Laboratory acid extraction at 121 °C; not a cooking recommendation or native-free-vitamer measurement; material: Chicken breast purchased in Turkey; cooking state not established; contains dietary input; In five adults with mitochondrial myopathy, niacin at 750–1,000 mg/day raised blood NAD⁺ and brought muscle NAD⁺ to the control level. That is medical dosing in a deficiency disease, not a result for adequate everyday intake, and attention was not measured. No numerical amount, free-vitamer pool, cooking yield, bioavailability or food-to-NAD effect inferred. Total niacin is not relabelled as either form. Nicotinic acid supports NAD precursor capacity; myopathy supplement responses are not transferred to ordinary foods or brain outcomes. Composition source

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Nicotinic acid is the Preiss–Handler substrate that cells convert toward NAD⁺.

Evidence source = https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC7963035/; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/32386566/

Limitation = In five adults with mitochondrial myopathy, niacin at 750–1,000 mg/day raised blood NAD⁺ and brought muscle NAD⁺ to the control level. That is medical dosing in a deficiency disease, not a result for adequate everyday intake, and attention was not measured. No numerical amount, free-vitamer pool, cooking yield, bioavailability or food-to-NAD effect inferred. Total niacin is not relabelled as either form. Nicotinic acid supports NAD precursor capacity; myopathy supplement responses are not transferred to ordinary foods or brain outcomes.

Dietary input = Yes

Chickpeas (studied analytical material)ⓘ — Laboratory acid extraction at 121 °C; not a cooking recommendation or native-free-vitamer measurement; material: Chickpea samples purchased in Turkey; cooking state not established; contains dietary input; In five adults with mitochondrial myopathy, niacin at 750–1,000 mg/day raised blood NAD⁺ and brought muscle NAD⁺ to the control level. That is medical dosing in a deficiency disease, not a result for adequate everyday intake, and attention was not measured. No numerical amount, free-vitamer pool, cooking yield, bioavailability or food-to-NAD effect inferred. Total niacin is not relabelled as either form. Nicotinic acid supports NAD precursor capacity; myopathy supplement responses are not transferred to ordinary foods or brain outcomes. Composition source

BRS3-FM2-PM3 — Sulforaphane

Food target and evidence

Input = Sulforaphane

Input type = regulatory modulator

Biological role = Modulates KEAP1-mediated control of Nrf2, promoting antioxidant-response gene transcription. Human dietary exposure increased Nrf2-associated phase II enzyme RNA in nasal lavage cells.

Evidence source = https://doi.org/10.1128/MCB.24.24.10941-10953.2004; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/19028145/; https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0163716

Limitation = Human target engagement varies by formulation, tissue and population. The positive study used a myrosinase-completed broccoli sprout homogenate for three days in healthy nonsmokers and measured nasal phase II enzyme RNA, not Nrf2 protein or nuclear translocation. Extract capsules did not increase lung target-gene expression in COPD. An ordinary broccoli meal, direct activation of Nrf2 in the human brain and clinical ADHD benefit remain unestablished. Sulforaphane is not an essential nutrient. Sulforaphane forms from glucoraphanin when sprouts are chopped or chewed; preparation affects the amount. Sulforaphane engages Keap1/Nrf2 biology; human nasal gene response depends on the tested preparation. Preserve the COPD null and food myrosinase dependence.

Dietary input = Yes

Broccoli Sprouts — raw; contains dietary input; Human target engagement varies by formulation, tissue and population. The positive study used a myrosinase-completed broccoli sprout homogenate for three days in healthy nonsmokers and measured nasal phase II enzyme RNA, not Nrf2 protein or nuclear translocation. Extract capsules did not increase lung target-gene expression in COPD. An ordinary broccoli meal, direct activation of Nrf2 in the human brain and clinical ADHD benefit remain unestablished. Sulforaphane is not an essential nutrient. Sulforaphane forms from glucoraphanin when sprouts are chopped or chewed; preparation affects the amount. Sulforaphane engages Keap1/Nrf2 biology; human nasal gene response depends on the tested preparation. Preserve the COPD null and food myrosinase dependence. Composition source

BRS3-FM2-PM4 — Selenium

Food target and evidence

Input = Selenium

Input type = cofactor

Biological role = Selenocysteine source for glutathione peroxidase, which clears hydroperoxides

Evidence source = https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC2921536/

Limitation = In selenium-deficient adults, plasma glutathione peroxidase activity levelled off once intake reached about 35 micrograms a day. Intake above that plateau did not raise the enzyme further, and the study did not measure net oxidative pressure. USDA FoodData Central reports selenium in Fish, anchovy, european, canned in oil, drained solids. Selenium sustains GPX capacity; selected deficiency/repletion and plateau are not whole-body oxidative-pressure or extra-intake benefit.

BRS3-FM2-PM4 — Copper

Food target and evidence

Input = Copper

Input type = cofactor

Biological role = Catalytic metal in copper-zinc superoxide dismutase, which converts superoxide to hydrogen peroxide

Evidence source = https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/3934352/

Limitation = Erythrocyte superoxide dismutase rose after copper repletion in infants with marginal copper status. That does not show that extra copper raises the enzyme when status is already adequate. USDA FoodData Central reports copper in Nuts, almonds. Copper supports SOD capacity; the infant repletion experiment is not evidence to raise intake in replete adults.

Dietary input = Yes

Almonds — preparation unresolved; contains dietary input; Erythrocyte superoxide dismutase rose after copper repletion in infants with marginal copper status. That does not show that extra copper raises the enzyme when status is already adequate. USDA FoodData Central reports copper in Nuts, almonds. Copper supports SOD capacity; the infant repletion experiment is not evidence to raise intake in replete adults. Composition source

BRS3-FM2-PM6 — Vitamin C

Food target and evidence

Input = Vitamin C

Input type = nutrient/substance

Biological role = Reduces the vitamin E radical so vitamin E can keep working. This is regeneration, not the chain-breaking job in the membrane.

Evidence source = https://www.nature.com/articles/278737a0

Limitation = Pulse radiolysis shows ascorbate reacting with the vitamin E radical. It does not show that more vitamin C speeds recycling in people. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin C in Apples, raw, with skin (Includes foods for USDA's Food Distribution Program). Ascorbate can reduce the tocopheroxyl radical; chemistry supports capacity, not a demonstrated high-dose recycling intervention.

Dietary input = Yes

Apples — raw; contains dietary input; Pulse radiolysis shows ascorbate reacting with the vitamin E radical. It does not show that more vitamin C speeds recycling in people. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin C in Apples, raw, with skin (Includes foods for USDA's Food Distribution Program). Ascorbate can reduce the tocopheroxyl radical; chemistry supports capacity, not a demonstrated high-dose recycling intervention. Composition source

BRS3-FM2-PM6 — Vitamin E

Food target and evidence

Input = Vitamin E

Input type = nutrient/substance

Biological role = The lipid-phase antioxidant whose radical is reduced by vitamin C. Chain-breaking in the membrane is a different job.

Evidence source = https://www.nature.com/articles/278737a0; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/17561088/

Limitation = Regeneration of the vitamin E radical is not a high-dose rule. A prevention trial found more prostate cancers with 400 IU of vitamin E a day. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin E in Nuts, almonds. Vitamin E participates in the regenerating pair; its PM5 chain-breaking role is separate and these mappings do not multiply intake needs.

Dietary input = Yes

Almonds — preparation unresolved; contains dietary input; Vitamin E breaks peroxyl-radical chains in membranes. That chemistry does not show that extra vitamin E lowers peroxidation in people. A prevention trial found more prostate cancers with 400 IU a day. USDA FoodData Central reports vitamin E in Nuts, almonds. Alpha-tocopherol-related lipid protection is a capacity relationship, not general antioxidant efficacy or a high-dose recommendation. Composition source

BRS5-FM2-PM6 — Ellagic acid

Food target and evidence

Pomegranate (studied commercial juice) → Ellagic acid: food ontology link pending.

Input = Ellagic acid

Input type = substrate

Biological role = Immediate microbial substrate for the assessed urolithin-generation pathway.

Evidence source = https://pubs.rsc.org/doi/c4fo00092g; https://doi.org/10.1038/s41430-021-00950-1

Limitation = Conversion depends on microbial community and may stop at intermediates. This does not imply universal conversion, increased mitochondrial function or benefit. Product and batch dependent. No urolithin A was detected in the juice; microbial conversion is variable. No whole-fruit quantity, ordinary-serving efficacy or brain benefit inferred. Ellagic acid is the immediate microbial substrate; only the assayed juice material is proposed provisionally, not all whole pomegranates.

Dietary input = Yes

Pomegranate (studied commercial juice)ⓘ — Chilled commercial juice; not whole-fruit or other-product equivalence; material: Commercial POM Wonderful 100% pomegranate juice batches assayed in Singh et al.; contains dietary input; Conversion depends on microbial community and may stop at intermediates. This does not imply universal conversion, increased mitochondrial function or benefit. Product and batch dependent. No urolithin A was detected in the juice; microbial conversion is variable. No whole-fruit quantity, ordinary-serving efficacy or brain benefit inferred. Ellagic acid is the immediate microbial substrate; only the assayed juice material is proposed provisionally, not all whole pomegranates. Composition source

BRS5-FM2-PM6 — Ellagitannins

Food target and evidence

Input = Ellagitannins

Input type = substrate provision

Biological role = Hydrolysis releases ellagic acid upstream of microbial urolithin formation.

Evidence source = https://pubs.rsc.org/doi/c4fo00092g; https://doi.org/10.1038/s41430-021-00950-1

Limitation = The human study tested a pomegranate-juice matrix rather than isolated equivalence of every ellagitannin; final metabolite generation varies. Assay-specific extraction. This is not an amount and not a urolithin A exposure. Ellagitannins supply ellagic acid upstream of microbial products; walnut composition does not establish predictable urolithin A or mitochondrial efficacy.

Dietary input = Yes

Walnuts — Walnut kernels; assay-specific extraction; supplies precursor; The human study tested a pomegranate-juice matrix rather than isolated equivalence of every ellagitannin; final metabolite generation varies. Assay-specific extraction. This is not an amount and not a urolithin A exposure. Ellagitannins supply ellagic acid upstream of microbial products; walnut composition does not establish predictable urolithin A or mitochondrial efficacy. Composition source Composition source

Biological role = Hydrolysis releases ellagic acid upstream of microbial urolithin formation.

Evidence source = https://pubs.rsc.org/doi/c4fo00092g; https://doi.org/10.1038/s41430-021-00950-1

Limitation = The human study tested a pomegranate-juice matrix rather than isolated equivalence of every ellagitannin; final metabolite generation varies. Product and batch dependent. No urolithin A was detected in the juice; microbial conversion is variable. No whole-fruit quantity, ordinary-serving efficacy or brain benefit inferred. Ellagitannins supply ellagic acid upstream of microbial products; walnut composition does not establish predictable urolithin A or mitochondrial efficacy.

Dietary input = Yes

Pomegranate (studied commercial juice)ⓘ — Chilled commercial juice; not whole-fruit or other-product equivalence; material: Commercial POM Wonderful 100% pomegranate juice batches assayed in Singh et al.; supplies precursor; The human study tested a pomegranate-juice matrix rather than isolated equivalence of every ellagitannin; final metabolite generation varies. Product and batch dependent. No urolithin A was detected in the juice; microbial conversion is variable. No whole-fruit quantity, ordinary-serving efficacy or brain benefit inferred. Ellagitannins supply ellagic acid upstream of microbial products; walnut composition does not establish predictable urolithin A or mitochondrial efficacy. Composition source
